PoS-on-GA input contract v1 — bounded handoff, 2026-10-18

Outcome: offline checks and a fresh zero-model source-bound Docker synthetic
certification passed. The one authorized new real PoS compatibility probe
FAILED at the original progress validator. None of the four formal tasks was
started. No external retry, prompt adjustment, model switch, or formal Task
authorization followed the failure. This is protocol evidence, not method
effectiveness evidence or a reproduction of the PoS paper result.

----------------------------

Method label: pos-ga-input-contract-v1 = fixed released PoS core + GA
transfer + pre-generation format/field interface supplement. It is NOT

but does not add an unverified provider response_format/output_config, force
a tool response, or loosen post-generation extraction. The previous strict
one-whole-object Markdown fence removal stays unchanged.

Supplement actually sent as an additional system message
--------------------------------------------------------
Version: pos-ga-input-contract-v1
All PoS role requests, including original JSON correction requests:
PoS JSON output contract: the entire public response must be exactly one complete JSON object. Do not use a Markdown code fence, explanatory text before or after it, or a second object.

Only original incremental-update-structure requests and their corrections,
including Sentinel candidate repair, additionally receive:
PoS incremental World record interface (the original requested update schema and validator remain authoritative): each upsert_entities item is an Entity with required entity_id, entity_type, name; attributes defaults to {}. Each upsert_states item is a State with required state_id, entity_id, description; probability defaults to 1.0, reason to null, source_type to "observed". Each upsert_relations item is a Relation with required relation_id, source_id, target_id, description; probability defaults to 0.5, reason to null, source_type to "observed". For Relation only, the original normalizer accepts source_entity_id/src_entity_id/source/src as aliases for source_id, target_entity_id/dst_entity_id/target/dst as aliases for target_id, and relation_type/relation/type as aliases for description. These are accepted aliases, not extra required fields.

The update classifier checks the original first user message's fixed
"Required JSON schema:" block for "upsert_entities". The original
build_belief_update_prompt retains that first message through
build_belief_repair_prompt, including Sentinel repair. Initialize,
textualize, consistency and progress receive only the first paragraph.
Original message objects are copied without editing; pos_role_input_contract
records the original messages, supplement, scope, and hashes, while
pos_role_request records the effective messages. Actual HTTP payloads are
in raw_compat_v3/probe/http_attempts.json. The full text is also frozen in
each prepared manifest/config and authorized_serial_scope.json; its hash is
recorded per request. In this probe: 7 object-only, 2 update-record requests.

Upstream basis: vendor/belief/worldStates.py Entity, State, Relation dataclass
constructors define the required fields and defaults; vendor/belief/manager.py
_validate_model_payload constructs these types, _normalize_model_payload maps
only the listed Relation aliases, _apply_world_update instantiates records,
and _request_json retains original correction count and schema validation.
vendor/prompts/prompt_generation.py supplies INITIAL_SCHEMA, UPDATE_SCHEMA,
build_belief_update_prompt and build_belief_repair_prompt. ID-prefix comments
and fields shown in initialization examples were not promoted into additional
required validation. This supplement gives no synthetic answer or task tactic.

+def compatibility_checks(rows, result, initial, updated, wire, roles,
+                         *, work_finished):
+    """Judge final original-core outcomes, not a transient parse error or HTTP 200."""
+    def has(event, step=None, operation=None):
+        return any(row.get('event') == event and
+                   (step is None or row.get('step') == step) and
+                   (operation is None or row.get('operation') == operation)
+                   for row in rows)
+    audit_submitted = [row for row in rows if
+        row.get('event') == 'belief_sentinel_audit_submitted' and row.get('step') == 0]
+    audit_completed = [row for row in rows if
+        row.get('event') == 'belief_sentinel_audit_completed' and row.get('step') == 0]
+    audit_failed = [row for row in rows if
+        row.get('event') == 'belief_sentinel_audit_failed' and row.get('step') == 0]
+    transitions = result.get('health_transitions') or []
+    progress = transitions[-1].get('progress') if len(transitions) == 1 else None
+    checks = {
+        'initial_original_parse_committed': has('belief_model_response', -1, 'initialize')
+            and has('belief_state_initialized', -1) and bool(initial.get('belief_text')),
+        'update_original_parse_resolved': has('belief_model_response', 0, 'update')
+            and has('belief_candidate_generated', 0)
+            and has('belief_state_updated', 0)
+            and len([item for item in result.get('belief_update_history') or []
+                     if item.get('step') == 0 and item.get('status') in
+                     {'validated', 'repaired', 'rejected'}]) == 1,
+        'sentinel_final_audit_resolved': bool(audit_submitted)
+            and len(audit_completed) == len(audit_submitted)
+            and not audit_failed
+            and len((result.get('belief_sentinel') or {}).get('audit_history') or [])
+                >= len(audit_completed),
+        'final_textualization': has('belief_model_response', 0, 'textualize')
+            and has('belief_text_updated', 0) and bool(updated.get('belief_text')),
+        'valid_nonterminal_progress': has('belief_sentinel_progress_completed', 0)
+            and has('belief_transition_progress_applied', 0)
+            and progress in (0, 1) and not isinstance(progress, bool)
+            and result.get('scored_transitions') == 1,
+        'applicable_work_finished': work_finished,
+        'role_and_transport_protocol': {'belief', 'consistency', 'progress'} <= set(roles)
+            and bool(wire) and all(row.get('http_status') == 200 and
+                row.get('returned_model') == 'claude-opus-4-8' and
+                row.get('stop_reason') not in {None, 'max_tokens'} for row in wire),
+    }
+    return {'status': 'passed' if all(checks.values()) else 'failed',
+            'checks': checks, 'audit_submitted': len(audit_submitted),
+            'audit_completed': len(audit_completed),
+            'audit_failed': len(audit_failed), 'transition_progress': progress}

     return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=True, separators=(',', ':'))
 
 
+def _role_input_contract(role, messages):
+    """Append an auditable adapter message without editing upstream messages.
+
+    Fixed upstream update and Sentinel repair prompts retain the first user
+    message's Required JSON schema through their bounded correction calls.
+    Textualize, initialize, consistency, and progress do not have that schema.
+    """
+    update = role == 'belief' and any(
+        item.get('role') == 'user' and
+        'Required JSON schema:' in item.get('content', '') and
+        '"upsert_entities"' in item['content'].split('Required JSON schema:', 1)[1]
+        for item in messages[:2]
+    )
+    supplement = ROLE_OBJECT_INSTRUCTION
+    if update:
+        supplement += '\n\n' + ROLE_UPDATE_FIELDS_INSTRUCTION
+    effective = [dict(item) for item in messages]
+    effective.append({'role': 'system', 'content': supplement})
+    return effective, supplement, 'update_records' if update else 'object_only'
+
+

+                      'messages': effective, 'status': 'attempted'}

+                             contract_version=ROLE_INPUT_CONTRACT_VERSION,

+                                 supplement.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest(),
+                             effective_messages_sha256=hashlib.sha256(
+                                 _json(effective).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest())

/opt/m4-runtime/python/cpython-3.12.12-linux-x86_64-gnu/bin/python3.12 -c 'import urllib.request; response = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({})).open("http://127.0.0.1:19173/", timeout=5); assert response.status == 200'
